# Graph Report - CivicBrain  (2026-09-20)

## Corpus Check
- 1100 files · ~3,249,220 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 19 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 8, .cmd 5, .toml 4)

## Summary
- 34381 nodes · 41741 edges · 2813 communities (2792 shown, 21 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 495 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `3c712f6e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
